theory RHTNCeremony

// Function signature and definition of the equational theory E

functions: fst/1, h/1, pair/2, pk/1, sign/2, snd/1, true/0, verify/3
equations:
    fst(<x.1, x.2>) = x.1,
    snd(<x.1, x.2>) = x.2,
    verify(sign(x.1, x.2), x.1, pk(x.2)) = true

rule (modulo E) Register:
   [ Fr( ~skc ), Fr( ~skp ) ]
  --[ Reg( $A ), OnlyOnce( $A ) ]->
   [
   !LtkC( $A, ~skc ), !LtkP( $A, ~skp ), !Kh( $A, h(<pk(~skc), pk(~skp)>) ),
   Out( <pk(~skc), pk(~skp)> )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Compromise_Classical:
   [ !LtkC( $A, ~skc ) ]
  --[ Compromised( $A ), CompromisedClassical( $A ) ]->
   [ Out( ~skc ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Compromise_PostQuantum:
   [ !LtkP( $A, ~skp ) ]
  --[ Compromised( $A ), CompromisedPQ( $A ) ]->
   [ Out( ~skp ) ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Meet:
   [ Fr( ~cid ) ]
  --[ MeetHappened( $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid ), DistinctParties( $P1, $P2, $W )
  ]->
   [
   CoPresent( $P1, ~cid ), CoPresent( $P2, ~cid ),
   !Ceremony( $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid ), Out( ~cid )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

restriction DistinctParties:
  "∀ a b w #i.
    (DistinctParties( a, b, w ) @ #i) ⇒
    (((¬(a = b)) ∧ (¬(a = w))) ∧ (¬(b = w)))"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ a b w #i.
    (DistinctParties( a, b, w ) @ #i) ⇒
    (((¬(a = b)) ∧ (¬(a = w))) ∧ (¬(b = w)))"
  */

                        <'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1.38, $P2.39, $W.40, cid.41>, x.73)
  */

rule (modulo E) Meet_Formation:
   [ Fr( ~cid ) ]
  --[ FormationMeetHappened( $P1, $P2, ~cid ), DistinctPair( $P1, $P2 ) ]->
   [
   CoPresent( $P1, ~cid ), CoPresent( $P2, ~cid ),
   !Formation( $P1, $P2, ~cid ), Out( ~cid )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

restriction DistinctPair:
  "∀ a b #i. (DistinctPair( a, b ) @ #i) ⇒ (¬(a = b))"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ a b #i. (DistinctPair( a, b ) @ #i) ⇒ (¬(a = b))"
  */

rule (modulo E) Formation_Sign:
   [
   !LtkC( $P, ~skc ), !LtkP( $P, ~skp ), CoPresent( $P, cid ),
   !Formation( $P1, $P2, cid )
   ]
  --[
  FormationSigned( $P, $P1, $P2, cid ),
  HonestFormationSigned( $P, $P1, $P2, cid ),
  SignedFormation( $P, $P1, $P2, cid )
  ]->
   [
   Out( <sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, cid>, ~skc), 
         sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, cid>, ~skp)>
   )
   ]

  /* has exactly the trivial AC variant */

rule (modulo E) Accept_Formation:
   [
   !Kh( $P1, kh1 ), !Kh( $P2, kh2 ), In( <km1, km2> ),
   In( <'formation', $P1, $P2, cid> ), In( <sig1c, sig1p> ),
   In( <sig2c, sig2p> )
   ]
  --[
  Eq( h(km1), kh1 ), Eq( h(km2), kh2 ),
  Eq( verify(sig1c, <'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, cid>, fst(km1)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(sig1p, <'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, cid>, snd(km1)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(sig2c, <'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, cid>, fst(km2)),
      true
  ),
  Eq( verify(sig2p, <'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, cid>, snd(km2)),
      true
  ),
  DistinctPair( $P1, $P2 ), FormationAccepted( $P1, $P2, cid )
  ]->
   [ ]

        km1   = <pk(x.48), pk(x.49)>
        km2   = <x.50, x.51>
        sig1c = sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.48)
        sig1p = sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.49)
        sig2c = sig2c.36
        sig2p = sig2p.37
        z     = true
        z.1   = true
        z.2   = verify(sig2c.36,
                       <'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.50)
        z.3   = verify(sig2p.37,
                       <'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.51)
    
    24. $P1   = $P1.27
        $P2   = $P2.28
        cid   = cid.29
        km1   = <pk(x.48), pk(x.49)>
        km2   = <x.50, pk(x.51)>
        sig1c = sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.48)
        sig1p = sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.49)
        sig2c = sig2c.36
        sig2p = sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.51)
        z     = true
        z.1   = true
        z.2   = verify(sig2c.36,
                       <'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.50)
        z.3   = true
    
    25. $P1   = $P1.27
        $P2   = $P2.28
        cid   = cid.29
        km1   = <pk(x.48), pk(x.49)>
        km2   = <pk(x.50), x.51>
        sig1c = sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.48)
        sig1p = sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.49)
        sig2c = sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.50)
        sig2p = sig2p.37

                       <'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1.27, $P2.28, cid.29>, x.51)
  */

restriction OneKeyPerName:
  "∀ a #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( a ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( a ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ a #i #j. ((OnlyOnce( a ) @ #i) ∧ (OnlyOnce( a ) @ #j)) ⇒ (#i = #j)"
  */

restriction Equality:
  "∀ x y #i. (Eq( x, y ) @ #i) ⇒ (x = y)"
  // safety formula

  /*
  expanded formula:
  "∀ x y #i. (Eq( x, y ) @ #i) ⇒ (x = y)"
  */

lemma ceremony_is_executable:
  exists-trace "∃ P1 P2 W cid #i. RecordAccepted( P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #i"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ P1 P2 W cid #i. (RecordAccepted( P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #i)"
*/
simplify
solve( !Kh( $P1, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₀ #i )
  case Register
  solve( !Kh( $P2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !Kh( $W, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, cid>, ~skc)
             ) @ #vk.15 )
        case Participant_Sign_Without_Meeting
        solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, cid>, ~skc.1)
               ) @ #vk.18 )
          case Participant_Sign
          solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid>, ~skc.2)
                 ) @ #vk.21 )
            case Participant_Sign_Without_Meeting
            solve( !KU( ~cid ) @ #vk.13 )
              case Meet
              solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid>, ~skp)
                     ) @ #vk.20 )
                case Participant_Sign_Without_Meeting
                solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid>, ~skp.1)
                       ) @ #vk.21 )
                  case Participant_Sign
                  solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid>, ~skp.2)
                         ) @ #vk.22 )
                    case Participant_Sign_Without_Meeting
                    solve( !KU( pk(~skc) ) @ #vk.23 )
                      case Register
                      solve( !KU( pk(~skp) ) @ #vk.24 )
                        case Register
                        solve( !KU( pk(~skc.1) ) @ #vk.25 )
                          case Register
                          solve( !KU( pk(~skp.1) ) @ #vk.26 )
                            case Register
                            solve( !KU( pk(~skc.2) ) @ #vk.27 )
                              case Register
                              solve( !KU( pk(~skp.2) ) @ #vk.28 )
                                case Register
                                SOLVED // trace found
                              qed
                            qed
                          qed
                        qed
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
qed

lemma honest_ceremony_completes:
  exists-trace
  "∃ P1 P2 W cid #m #a #b #i.
    ((((((((MeetHappened( P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #m) ∧
           (HonestParticipantSigned( P1, P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #a)) ∧
          (HonestParticipantSigned( P2, P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #b)) ∧
         (RecordAccepted( P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #i)) ∧
        (#m < #a)) ∧
       (#m < #b)) ∧
      (#a < #i)) ∧
     (#b < #i)) ∧
    (¬(∃ A #k. Compromised( A ) @ #k))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ P1 P2 W cid #m #a #b #i.
  (MeetHappened( P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #m) ∧
  (HonestParticipantSigned( P1, P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #a) ∧
  (HonestParticipantSigned( P2, P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #b) ∧
  (RecordAccepted( P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (#m < #a) ∧
  (#m < #b) ∧
  (#a < #i) ∧
  (#b < #i) ∧
  (∀ A #k. (Compromised( A ) @ #k) ⇒ ⊥)"
*/
simplify
solve( !LtkC( $P1, ~skc ) ▶₀ #a )
  case Register
  solve( !LtkP( $P1, ~skp ) ▶₁ #a )
    case Register
    solve( CoPresent( $P1, ~cid ) ▶₂ #a )
      case Meet_case_1
      solve( !Ceremony( $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid ) ▶₃ #a )
        case Meet
        solve( !LtkC( $P2, ~skc.1 ) ▶₀ #b )
          case Register
          solve( !LtkP( $P2, ~skp.1 ) ▶₁ #b )
            case Register
            solve( CoPresent( $P2, ~cid ) ▶₂ #b )
              case Meet_case_2
              solve( !Ceremony( $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid ) ▶₃ #b )
                case Meet
                solve( !Kh( $P1, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₀ #i )
                  case Register
                  solve( !Kh( $P2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
                    case Register
                    solve( !Kh( $W, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
                      case Register
                      solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid>, ~skc.2)
                             ) @ #vk.21 )
                        case Participant_Sign_Without_Meeting
                        solve( !KU( ~cid ) @ #vk.9 )
                          case Meet
                          solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid>, ~skc)
                                 ) @ #vk.17 )
                            case Participant_Sign_Without_Meeting
                            solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid>, ~skp)
                                   ) @ #vk.18 )
                              case Participant_Sign_Without_Meeting
                              solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid>, ~skc.1)
                                     ) @ #vk.20 )
                                case Participant_Sign
                                solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid>, ~skp.1)
                                       ) @ #vk.21 )
                                  case Participant_Sign
                                  solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, ~cid>, ~skp.2)
                                         ) @ #vk.22 )
                                    case Participant_Sign_Without_Meeting
                                    solve( !KU( pk(~skc) ) @ #vk.23 )
                                      case Register
                                      solve( !KU( pk(~skp) ) @ #vk.24 )
                                        case Register
                                        solve( !KU( pk(~skc.1) ) @ #vk.25 )
                                          case Register
                                          solve( !KU( pk(~skp.1) ) @ #vk.26 )
                                            case Register
                                            solve( !KU( pk(~skc.2) ) @ #vk.27 )
                                              case Register
                                              solve( !KU( pk(~skp.2) ) @ #vk.28 )
                                                case Register
                                                SOLVED // trace found
                                              qed
                                            qed
                                          qed
                                        qed
                                      qed
                                    qed
                                  qed
                                qed
                              qed
                            qed
                          qed
                        qed
                      qed
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed

lemma honest_formation_completes:
  exists-trace
  "∃ P1 P2 cid #m #a #b #i.
    ((((((((FormationMeetHappened( P1, P2, cid ) @ #m) ∧
           (HonestFormationSigned( P1, P1, P2, cid ) @ #a)) ∧
          (HonestFormationSigned( P2, P1, P2, cid ) @ #b)) ∧
         (FormationAccepted( P1, P2, cid ) @ #i)) ∧
        (#m < #a)) ∧
       (#m < #b)) ∧
      (#a < #i)) ∧
     (#b < #i)) ∧
    (¬(∃ A #k. Compromised( A ) @ #k))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ P1 P2 cid #m #a #b #i.
  (FormationMeetHappened( P1, P2, cid ) @ #m) ∧
  (HonestFormationSigned( P1, P1, P2, cid ) @ #a) ∧
  (HonestFormationSigned( P2, P1, P2, cid ) @ #b) ∧
  (FormationAccepted( P1, P2, cid ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (#m < #a) ∧
  (#m < #b) ∧
  (#a < #i) ∧
  (#b < #i) ∧
  (∀ A #k. (Compromised( A ) @ #k) ⇒ ⊥)"
*/
simplify
solve( !LtkC( $P1, ~skc ) ▶₀ #a )
  case Register
  solve( !LtkP( $P1, ~skp ) ▶₁ #a )
    case Register
    solve( CoPresent( $P1, ~cid ) ▶₂ #a )
      case Meet_Formation_case_1
      solve( !Formation( $P1, $P2, ~cid ) ▶₃ #a )
        case Meet_Formation
        solve( !LtkC( $P2, ~skc.1 ) ▶₀ #b )
          case Register
          solve( !LtkP( $P2, ~skp.1 ) ▶₁ #b )
            case Register
            solve( CoPresent( $P2, ~cid ) ▶₂ #b )
              case Meet_Formation_case_2
              solve( !Formation( $P1, $P2, ~cid ) ▶₃ #b )
                case Meet_Formation
                solve( !Kh( $P1, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₀ #i )
                  case Register
                  solve( !Kh( $P2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
                    case Register
                    solve( !KU( ~cid ) @ #vk.9 )
                      case Meet_Formation
                      solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, ~cid>, ~skc)
                             ) @ #vk.11 )
                        case Formation_Sign_Without_Meeting
                        solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, ~cid>, ~skp)
                               ) @ #vk.12 )
                          case Formation_Sign_Without_Meeting
                          solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, ~cid>, ~skc.1)
                                 ) @ #vk.14 )
                            case Formation_Sign
                            solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, ~cid>, ~skp.1)
                                   ) @ #vk.15 )
                              case Formation_Sign
                              solve( !KU( pk(~skc) ) @ #vk.16 )
                                case Register
                                solve( !KU( pk(~skp) ) @ #vk.17 )
                                  case Register
                                  solve( !KU( pk(~skc.1) ) @ #vk.18 )
                                    case Register
                                    solve( !KU( pk(~skp.1) ) @ #vk.19 )
                                      case Register
                                      SOLVED // trace found
                                    qed
                                  qed
                                qed
                              qed
                            qed
                          qed
                        qed

lemma an_accepted_record_is_attributable:
  all-traces
  "∀ P1 P2 W cid #i.
    (RecordAccepted( P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #i) ⇒
    ((((∃ #a. (SignedBody( P1, P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #a) ∧ (#a < #i)) ∨
       ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P1 ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
        (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P1 ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i)))) ∧
      ((∃ #b. (SignedBody( P2, P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i)) ∨
       ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P2 ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
        (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P2 ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i))))) ∧
     ((∃ #w. (SignedBody( W, P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #w) ∧ (#w < #i)) ∨
      ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( W ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
       (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( W ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i)))))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P1 P2 W cid #i.
  (RecordAccepted( P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (((∀ #a. (SignedBody( P1, P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #a) ⇒ ¬(#a < #i)) ∧
    (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P1 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
      (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P1 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))) ∨
   ((∀ #b. (SignedBody( P2, P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i)) ∧
    (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P2 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
      (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P2 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))) ∨
   ((∀ #w. (SignedBody( W, P1, P2, W, cid ) @ #w) ⇒ ¬(#w < #i)) ∧
    (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( W ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
      (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( W ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))))"
*/
simplify
solve( ((∀ #a.
          (SignedBody( $P1, $P1, $P2, $W, cid ) @ #a) ⇒ ¬(#a < #i)) ∧
        (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( $P1 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
          (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( $P1 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)))))  ∥
       ((∀ #b. (SignedBody( $P2, $P1, $P2, $W, cid ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i)) ∧
        (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( $P2 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
          (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( $P2 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)))))  ∥
       ((∀ #w. (SignedBody( $W, $P1, $P2, $W, cid ) @ #w) ⇒ ¬(#w < #i)) ∧
        (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( $W ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
          (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( $W ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))) )
  case case_1
  solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( $P1 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
         (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( $P1 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
    case case_1
    solve( !Kh( $P1, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $P2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $W, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, cid>, ~skc)
                 ) @ #vk.15 )
            case Participant_Sign_Without_Meeting
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          next
            case Participant_Sign_case_1
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          next
            case Participant_Sign_case_2
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          next
            case Witness_Sign
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          next
            case c_sign
            solve( !KU( ~skc ) @ #vk.31 )
              case Compromise_Classical
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  next
    case case_2
    solve( !Kh( $P1, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $P2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $W, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
          case Register

            by contradiction /* from formulas */

            by contradiction /* from formulas */

            by contradiction /* from formulas */

                by contradiction /* from formulas */

                by contradiction /* from formulas */

                by contradiction /* from formulas */

            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
next
  case case_2
  solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( $P2 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
         (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( $P2 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
    case case_1

      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $P2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !Kh( $W, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₂ #i )
          case Register
          solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, cid>, ~skc)

            case Participant_Sign_Without_Meeting
            solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '0', $P1, $P2, $W, cid>, ~skc.1)

              case Participant_Sign

lemma formation_is_executable:
  exists-trace "∃ P1 P2 cid #i. FormationAccepted( P1, P2, cid ) @ #i"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all satisfying traces:
"∃ P1 P2 cid #i. (FormationAccepted( P1, P2, cid ) @ #i)"
*/
simplify
solve( !Kh( $P1, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₀ #i )
  case Register
  solve( !Kh( $P2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
    case Register
    solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, cid>, ~skc)
           ) @ #vk.11 )
      case Formation_Sign_Without_Meeting
      solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, cid>, ~skc.1)
             ) @ #vk.14 )
        case Formation_Sign
        solve( !KU( ~cid ) @ #vk.12 )
          case Meet_Formation
          solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, ~cid>, ~skp)
                 ) @ #vk.14 )
            case Formation_Sign_Without_Meeting
            solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, ~cid>, ~skp.1)
                   ) @ #vk.15 )
              case Formation_Sign
              solve( !KU( pk(~skc) ) @ #vk.16 )
                case Register
                solve( !KU( pk(~skp) ) @ #vk.17 )
                  case Register
                  solve( !KU( pk(~skc.1) ) @ #vk.18 )
                    case Register
                    solve( !KU( pk(~skp.1) ) @ #vk.19 )
                      case Register
                      SOLVED // trace found
                    qed
                  qed
                qed
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
qed

lemma an_accepted_formation_is_attributable:
  all-traces
  "∀ P1 P2 cid #i.
    (FormationAccepted( P1, P2, cid ) @ #i) ⇒
    (((∃ #a. (SignedFormation( P1, P1, P2, cid ) @ #a) ∧ (#a < #i)) ∨
      ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P1 ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
       (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P1 ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i)))) ∧
     ((∃ #b. (SignedFormation( P2, P1, P2, cid ) @ #b) ∧ (#b < #i)) ∨
      ((∃ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P2 ) @ #x) ∧ (#x < #i)) ∧
       (∃ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P2 ) @ #y) ∧ (#y < #i)))))"
/*
guarded formula characterizing all counter-examples:
"∃ P1 P2 cid #i.
  (FormationAccepted( P1, P2, cid ) @ #i)
 ∧
  (((∀ #a. (SignedFormation( P1, P1, P2, cid ) @ #a) ⇒ ¬(#a < #i)) ∧
    (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P1 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
      (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P1 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))) ∨
   ((∀ #b. (SignedFormation( P2, P1, P2, cid ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i)) ∧
    (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( P2 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
      (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( P2 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))))"
*/
simplify
solve( ((∀ #a.
          (SignedFormation( $P1, $P1, $P2, cid ) @ #a) ⇒ ¬(#a < #i)) ∧
        (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( $P1 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
          (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( $P1 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)))))  ∥
       ((∀ #b. (SignedFormation( $P2, $P1, $P2, cid ) @ #b) ⇒ ¬(#b < #i)) ∧
        (((∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( $P2 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i)) ∨
          (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( $P2 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i))))) )
  case case_1
  solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( $P1 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
         (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( $P1 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
    case case_1
    solve( !Kh( $P1, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $P2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, cid>, ~skc)
               ) @ #vk.11 )
          case Formation_Sign_Without_Meeting
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case Formation_Sign_case_1
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case Formation_Sign_case_2
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case c_sign
          solve( !KU( ~skc ) @ #vk.22 )
            case Compromise_Classical
            by contradiction /* from formulas */
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  next
    case case_2
    solve( !Kh( $P1, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₀ #i )
      case Register
      solve( !Kh( $P2, h(<pk(x), pk(x.1)>) ) ▶₁ #i )
        case Register
        solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, cid>, ~skc)
               ) @ #vk.11 )
          case Formation_Sign_Without_Meeting
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case Formation_Sign_case_1
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case Formation_Sign_case_2
          by contradiction /* from formulas */
        next
          case c_sign
          solve( !KU( ~skc ) @ #vk.22 )
            case Compromise_Classical
            solve( !KU( sign(<'rhtn/1:envelope', '1', $P1, $P2, cid>, ~skp)
                   ) @ #vk.15 )
              case Formation_Sign_Without_Meeting
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case Formation_Sign_case_1
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case Formation_Sign_case_2
              by contradiction /* from formulas */
            next
              case c_sign
              solve( !KU( ~skp ) @ #vk.25 )
                case Compromise_PostQuantum
                by contradiction /* from formulas */
              qed
            qed
          qed
        qed
      qed
    qed
  qed
next
  case case_2
  solve( (∀ #x. (CompromisedClassical( $P2 ) @ #x) ⇒ ¬(#x < #i))  ∥
         (∀ #y. (CompromisedPQ( $P2 ) @ #y) ⇒ ¬(#y < #i)) )
